# SP115_CW_156-7 — changes and adjudications

The final reading is determined from the supplied ink. Each row is one adjudicated place; a row may differ from both witnesses. Macro syntax alone is excluded. Doubt decisions are retained as evidence but distinguished from substantive text/state differences. Added uncertainty alone belongs in the doubts file.

Counts in the C/H columns are the explicitly adjudicated differences recorded in the corresponding Boolean fields of the per-page final records. Resolutions that leave the same primary letters and source state can be recorded without counting as a substantive difference. Confidence describes the final ink decision, not the mathematical truth of the statement.

## Consequential corrections

- Scan 002, scan002-16, Body line 7, first parenthetical property: <code>densité</code>. The ending contains the separate i tick, a tall t stroke with cross-stroke, and an accented e. It does not have the -mension sequence of dimension.
- Scan 002, scan002-21, Body line 11, combinatoires fines: <code>fines</code>. The crop shows f, a single i, compressed n-e, and the terminal s. There is no second i or separate dot between n and e.
- Scan 003, S003-04, eight-line diagonal marginal note, beside main lines 8–20: <code>L&#x27;autre raison, c&#x27;est que, dans le cas d&#x27;une ``application $f$&#x27;&#x27; d&#x27;une ``région&#x27;&#x27; dans une autre, l&#x27;image inverse d&#x27;une ``figure&#x27;&#x27; (\unsure{quand&#124;quant} \unsure{on} peut donner un sens \unsure{géométrique} \unsure{à} \unsure{ce} \unsure{mot&#124;terme}) \deleted{\unsure{sera&#124;devient&#124;devant}} tend à ne \unsure{pas} être \unsure{encore} une figure, mais l&#x27;image réciproque d&#x27;une multistr. est une multistr.</code>. The rotated horizontal baselines of the marginal note show L’autre raison, c’est que, dans le cas d’une; the long quoted word has the pp descenders, l ascender and -cation sequence of application, followed by a separate f, rather than localité/localisation. The next quoted group has the r lead-in, descending g and ending -ion of région; it continues dans une autre, not dans un site. The quote ticks around figure and the parenthesis remain visible among the crossing body strokes. Inside it, peut donner un sens is readable: the looped d and connected -onner exclude être, while géométrique, à, ce and mot/terme remain explicitly doubtful. After the cancellation, the upright crossed t and en-d strokes support tend; the following à ne, doubtful pas and encore, and être precede une figure. This is not a clearly written tandis que. The lower line shows mais l’image réciproque d’une, with the p descender and intervening c-r-o group; it is not a second inverse. The two multistr. forms stop after str and have abbreviation points, rather than fully written multistrate. The short est and une are retained, rather than sera encore un. No line-break command is source ink. Punctuation and existing doubts are retained; the cancelled-word alternatives are enlarged as separately checked in B005.

### Remaining uncertainties

- Upper-right corner classification: <code>GF</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;CF&#x27;]</code>; medium. The second initial’s two horizontal members favour F. The first open loop has a lower inward return, but its status as G rather than a connected C cannot be made wholly certain from this page; crop crops/scan002_corner.png, original box [1420,22,1664,121].
- Slanted reference note, underwriting beneath 50: <code>\unsure{50}</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;50 with retracing&#x27;, &#x27;50 over an unrecoverable earlier entry&#x27;]</code>; low. 50 is readable. Additional overlapping strokes cannot be separated into an earlier numeral, and an actual cancellation cannot be distinguished from retracing. The in-place doubt flags this source-state issue; no invented deleted word or numeral is supplied. Crop crops/scan002_references.png [1367,111,1664,337].
- Slanted reference note, retraced 58: <code>\unsure{58}</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;58 with retracing&#x27;, &#x27;58 over an unrecoverable earlier entry&#x27;]</code>; low. 23 is still legible: the long intervening upright descends from Cf, not a third digit. The retraced 50 and 58 are retained, with an added doubt on the source state of 58. The oval encloses 110-113 and its arrow points to the gap after 23. All alternate TikZ coordinates encode that same observed arrangement. No extra comma after 23 or 59 is supplied. New blind crop crops/second_scan002_B004.png; original box [1367, 111, 1664, 337].
- Body line 12, simplexe(s): <code>simplexe</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;simplexes&#x27;, &#x27;simplex&#x27;]</code>; medium. The crossed x is followed by a low terminal stroke before the separate full stop. Its segmentation as e, e+s, or the final arm of x remains uncertain; add simplex to the existing alternatives without silently normalising the spelling. The page already carries the final period. New blind crop crops/second_scan002_B019.png; original box [248, 1335, 610, 1443].
- Body line 20, verb after Ils: <code>pourront</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;pourraient&#x27;, &#x27;pourrait&#x27;]</code>; low. The p descender and ou-rr group are visible, but the low middle/end strokes do not securely distinguish -ont from -aient or -ait. The separate dot does not have an unambiguous i-stem partner. Preserve pourront as primary and explicitly retain the blind alternatives. New blind crop crops/second_scan002_B025.png; original box [1194, 2054, 1472, 2151].
- Body line 20, word before the comma: <code>sans</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;donc&#x27;]</code>; low. The opening low loop and two short rises are compressed enough to permit sans or donc; the detached descending comma at the right is clear. Retain sans first, add donc as alternative, and transcribe the comma. New blind crop crops/second_scan002_B026.png; original box [1481, 2065, 1611, 2129].
- Body line 21, before un rôle: <code>doute</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;donc&#x27;, &#x27;dans&#x27;]</code>; low. The initial looped d is plain. The flattened central run and tight final crossed/looped group favour doute but do not exclude donc or dans on this crop. The uncertainty is restored instead of relying on the familiar phrase sans doute. New blind crop crops/second_scan002_B027.png; original box [260, 2155, 441, 2231].

### Remaining uncertainties

- margin line 4: <code>quand</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;quant&#x27;]</code>; medium. The terminal ascender lies in overlapping diagonal and horizontal ink; the distinction d/t remains insufficiently plain.
- margin line 4: <code>on</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. The short group after quand lies amid crossing main-text strokes; the two-letter reading remains plausible but not fully separable.
- margin line 5: <code>géométrique</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. The initial/descending forms and overall sequence fit, but central letters cross the larger horizontal writing.
- margin line 5: <code>à</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. A very short group beside the crowded final part of the parenthesis; accent and base cannot both be isolated confidently.
- margin line 5: <code>ce</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. Small connected strokes overlap the large body hand; retained from C without asserting certainty.
- margin line 5: <code>mot</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;terme&#x27;]</code>; low. The final word and closing parenthesis occupy an overlap. Individual letters do not securely decide the two C alternatives.
- margin line 6: <code>pas</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. The apparent p and short following strokes cross main-text forms; the complete word is not fully separable.
- margin line 6: <code>encore</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. The low connected middle letters are crossed by larger horizontal ink; the C doubt is retained.
- main line 17, first M; recurring line 20: <code>M</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;\\mathcal M&#x27;]</code>; low. The two bare capitals are consistent with each other, but ordinary cursive M versus intentional calligraphic M cannot be decided securely from this page. The first occurrence remains marked; the second uses the first candidate.
- main line 25, struck word after F,: <code>il</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;le&#x27;]</code>; low. The compact cancellation covers the diagnostic strokes. Neither candidate can be read letter by letter.
- margin line 6, struck word: <code>sera</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;devient&#x27;, &#x27;devant&#x27;]</code>; low. Multiple cancellation strokes obscure the initial letters and central joining strokes. The low final group favours sera but does not let every letter exclude devient/devant. Keep the deletion and add the blind alternatives; do not resolve cancelled ink from syntax. New blind crop crops/second_scan003_B005.png; original box [28, 1558, 125, 1672].

|---|---|---|---|---|
| B004 | <code>\margin{L&#x27;autre raison, c&#x27;est que, dans le cas d&#x27;une ``application $f$&#x27;&#x27; d&#x27;une ``région&#x27;&#x27; dans une autre, l&#x27;image inverse d&#x27;une ``figure&#x27;&#x27; (\unsure{quand&#124;quant} \unsure{on} peut donner un sens \unsure{géométrique} \unsure{à} \unsure{ce} \unsure{mot&#124;terme}) \deleted{\unsure{sera}} tend à ne \unsure{pas} être \unsure{encore} une figure, mais l&#x27;image réciproque d&#x27;une multistr. est une multistr.}</code> | <code>\margin{L&#x27;autre raison, c&#x27;est que dans le\\ cas d&#x27;une ``\unsure{localité&#124;localisation}&#x27;&#x27; d&#x27;un\\ ``\illegible{1}&#x27;&#x27; \unsure{dans} un \unsure{site}, l&#x27;image\\ inverse d&#x27;une figure \illegible{1}\\ peut \unsure{être} un \illegible{2}\\ \deleted{\unsure{devient&#124;devant}} \unsure{tandis} \unsure{que} \illegible{2}\\ figure, mais l&#x27;image inverse d&#x27;un\\ multistrate \unsure{sera encore un} \unsure{multistrate&#124;multistr.}.}</code> | <code>\margin{L&#x27;autre raison, c&#x27;est que, dans le cas d&#x27;une ``application $f$&#x27;&#x27; d&#x27;une ``région&#x27;&#x27; dans une autre, l&#x27;image inverse d&#x27;une ``figure&#x27;&#x27; (\unsure{quand&#124;quant} \unsure{on} peut donner un sens \unsure{géométrique} \unsure{à} \unsure{ce} \unsure{mot&#124;terme}) \deleted{\unsure{sera&#124;devient&#124;devant}} tend à ne \unsure{pas} être \unsure{encore} une figure, mais l&#x27;image réciproque d&#x27;une multistr. est une multistr.}</code> | The rotated horizontal baselines of the marginal note show L’autre raison, c’est que, dans le cas d’une; the long quoted word has the pp descenders, l ascender and -cation sequence of application, followed by a separate f, rather than localité/localisation. The next quoted group has the r lead-in, descending g and ending -ion of région; it continues dans une autre, not dans un site. The quote ticks around figure and the parenthesis remain visible among the crossing body strokes. Inside it, peut donner un sens is readable: the looped d and connected -onner exclude être, while géométrique, à, ce and mot/terme remain explicitly doubtful. After the cancellation, the upright crossed t and en-d strokes support tend; the following à ne, doubtful pas and encore, and être precede une figure. This is not a clearly written tandis que. The lower line shows mais l’image réciproque d’une, with the p descender and intervening c-r-o group; it is not a second inverse. The two multistr. forms stop after str and have abbreviation points, rather than fully written multistrate. The short est and une are retained, rather than sera encore un. No line-break command is source ink. Punctuation and existing doubts are retained; the cancelled-word alternatives are enlarged as separately checked in B005. Crop: crops/second_scan003_B004.png; original box [15, 645, 1005, 1845]. Change-place IDs: 003-A01. |

### Remaining uncertainties

- lieux L, repeated Fig(L): <code>L</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;mathcal L&#x27;]</code>; low. The looped foot is compatible with either italic or calligraphic L. Crop scan004_lieux_deleted.png; repeated forms were checked in both left notes. First-occurrence C doubt retained.
- struck word after L: <code>ou</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;de&#x27;, &#x27;ce&#x27;]</code>; low. Only one small loop and fused low strokes survive the strike. Not every letter is plain; preserve C’s doubt and admit H’s ce without adopting it.
- heading, prefix and internal cancellation: <code>\unsure{pre&#124;pré}\deleted{\unsure{l&#124;li}}mières</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;possible accent above initial e&#x27;, &#x27;one or two cancelled letters at the junction&#x27;]</code>; low. Live pre and final mières surround the cancellation. Its exact underlying letters and overwritten accent remain unclear. This corrects C’s deletion boundary without claiming to recover every cancelled stroke. Crop crops/scan004_heading.png [348,728,892,831].
- defining ensemble-of-figures symbol: <code>mathscr F</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;mathcal F&#x27;]</code>; medium. A looped script F is visible; exact script/calligraphic font mapping is not resolved. The source does not have a plainly angular mathfrak form. First occurrence remains marked.
- inclusion label spelling: <code>rellation</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;relation&#x27;]</code>; low. Repeated tall loops after re cannot be safely segmented from the a on the crop. No spelling normalization.
- inclusion label ending before figures: <code>de</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;des&#x27;]</code>; low. The final horizontal run could contain s or be an extended e terminal. The grammar does not decide.
- label of first equivalence, whole F≤G iff F sous figure de G formula: <code>déf</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;def&#x27;]</code>; low. The cramped label is entangled with the descender/nearby line above. No safely isolated accent stroke decides it; the whole formula was cropped and read.
- row (a), mark following existe: <code>)</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;}&#x27;]</code>; low. The mark has a curled or crossed upper lobe and lower right-parenthesis curve. It may be a malformed or overwritten parenthesis or a brace. C doubt retained.
- struck heading of first left marginal note: <code>Question</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. The initial Q and some low joins survive, but the thick horizontal stroke obscures interior letters. The rule for doubtful deleted words forbids removing the doubt.
- row (b), Inf^≤(F,c/G), and recurring second argument in row (c): <code>c</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;G&#x27;]</code>; low. Small open curve, with no secure inward bar. G on this page can have a C-like body; exact case/letter is not decided. First recurrence in (b) remains marked; (c) uses preferred c.
- row (b), stroke before overlined m: <code>(</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;et (&#x27;]</code>; low. The tall curved stroke has a crossed or hooked lower end. It could be only a parenthesis or incorporate a compressed et. Retain C doubt.
- row (c) circled label: <code>c</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. The cramped inner strokes touch the surrounding circle, and the shape is not confidently separable. Retain C doubt.
- short struck group between (c) and (d): <code>G</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;CG&#x27;]</code>; low. Crossing strokes obscure the group. Some ink could suggest an additional first character, but not every letter is plain; C doubt remains.
- row (d) circled label: <code>d</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Inner loop/stem are crowded against the left side of the enclosing circle. Retain C doubt.
- opening phrase before suis resté muet: <code>je</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;y&#x27;]</code>; low. The compressed first group has a descending left stem and joined upper/right curve. These can be je with a flattened e or a single y-shaped form; the identifying separation is not secure. Retain je first with y as an alternative. The strokes of suis resté muet are unchanged.
- opening block, abbreviation before des lieux: <code>ens.</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;un.&#x27;]</code>; low. The abbreviation after l’ has very flattened joined humps and a following point. Ens. remains plausible, but the same compressed outline permits un.; preserve both. The L loop cannot settle ordinary versus calligraphic font at its first occurrence. The crossed following group preserves a loop and fused low strokes; not every letter is plain, so ou/de/ce remain rather than resolving to ou.
- parenthetical phrase before signifie: <code>cela</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;cel&#x27;]</code>; low. The opening group has c/e curves and a tall l loop, followed by a flat exit. A compressed terminal a may be merged into that exit, but cel cannot be excluded. Retain cela first and cel as an alternative; signifie qu’on a un is unchanged.
- bottom-left marginal note, opening verb: <code>Exist</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;Existe&#x27;]</code>; low. After Exis the tall crossed t ends without a separately recoverable final e loop; Exist is the better literal reading, while a compressed Existe remains possible. The final adjective has an m-like onset, a connected medial loop/ascender, dotted i and accented ending. A compressed tr sequence cannot be excluded from that middle cluster, so add métrisé beside mechisé/méchisé rather than deciding by vocabulary.
- bottom-left marginal word: <code>mechisé</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;méchisé&#x27;, &#x27;métrisé&#x27;]</code>; low. After Exis the tall crossed t ends without a separately recoverable final e loop; Exist is the better literal reading, while a compressed Existe remains possible. The final adjective has an m-like onset, a connected medial loop/ascender, dotted i and accented ending. A compressed tr sequence cannot be excluded from that middle cluster, so add métrisé beside mechisé/méchisé rather than deciding by vocabulary.
- empty figure description, word after plus petit: <code>élément</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;élémt&#x27;]</code>; low. The neutral crop clipped the last word, so a single root blind crop extends it to the physical sheet edge. The accented initial é, tall l and following é are visible; the rest is a flattened run followed by t. It admits both full élément and abbreviated élémt. The final t is not securely raised, so an unqualified superscript abbreviation is not adopted. Mark the word doubtful. The ambient-F alphabet is already governed by its first-occurrence doubt; both inequalities, parentheses and underlined et agree.

### Remaining uncertainties

- top page-number overwrite: <code>3</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Earlier rounded digit strokes are partly covered by the final 4; the overwritten reading is not fully plain.
- opening quotation marks before figures: <code>``</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. The opening pair is crowded against the tall f and the top boundary of this writing; the source quote form is plausible but retained as doubtful.
- upper right note, cancelled symbol after par: <code>\mathscr F</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Compact crossing strokes obscure the small underlying letter.
- cancelled word below L entry: <code>minimaux</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Several letters are obscured by multiple long cancelling strokes.
- first cancelled group in axiom introduction: <code>At 1</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;At 4&#x27;]</code>; low. Digit strokes are crossed; 1 and 4 remain possible.
- second cancelled group in axiom introduction: <code>et</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;4&#x27;]</code>; low. The overlap does not decide a short word versus numeral.
- At 2, overwritten initial before est: <code>d</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Underlying loop/ascender cannot be completely separated from the live e.
- cancelled short word below c), after live opening parenthesis: <code>et</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. The horizontal strike conceals the letters; the opening parenthesis itself is not crossed.
- union subscript in c): <code>\in</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;+&#x27;]</code>; medium. The small middle mark has insufficient separated strokes to distinguish membership from a plus; the whole union expression was read.
- last word of second line of lower marginal note: <code>équivaut</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. The ending is light and compressed, with thin strokes merging into the exit.
- cancelled word before se reconstitue: <code>est</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Several oblique cancellation strokes cover its beginning and middle.
- cancelled baseline word beneath lieux: <code>éléments</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;points&#x27;]</code>; low. The crossing covers the middle of the underlined word. The surviving lower oval and short descent permit points as well as éléments; neither is clear enough to resolve. Preserve the independent underline within the deletion.
- overwritten symbol under Phi inside b′) set brace: <code>F</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;A&#x27;]</code>; low. A lower triangular outline and transverse stroke permit an old A as well as F under the live capital Phi. The overlap prevents resolving either. Dollar delimiters add no source information.
- cancelled glyph before Dans in a′): <code>\forall</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;F&#x27;, &#x27;\\forall F&#x27;]</code>; low. The crossed upper inverted-V/bar and lower upright/bar residue cannot securely distinguish forall, F or a two-symbol forall F group. Preserve all three possibilities; math-mode wrapper is immaterial.
- superscript label over defining equivalence: <code>dfn</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;défn&#x27;, &#x27;defn&#x27;]</code>; low. The d loop, long f and terminal n-like turn support dfn first. A compressed e or accent cannot be excluded, so défn and defn remain alternatives; the blind déf would omit the visible terminal turn. Roman/text wrappers do not add a character.

Reproduce these strokes graphically, without assigning an alphabetic list label. Crop: crops/second_scan005_B002.png; original box [330, 72, 375, 111]. Change-place IDs: 005-A01. |
| B003 | <code>\textcircled{\scriptsize\unsure{e}} $M={}$ ensemble des \unsure{``}\uline{figures} $\subset\mathscr F$ \uline{élémentaires}&#x27;&#x27; ou \uline{multistrates}</code> | <code>\tikz[baseline=-.4ex,x=.75em,y=.75em]{\draw (.5,.5) circle (.48);\draw (.18,.48)--(.82,.48) (.4,.2)--(.65,.8) (.32,.76)--(.72,.23);} $M=$ ensemble des \uline{figures} $\in\unsure{\mathscr F&#124;\mathcal F}$ \uline{élémentaires}&#x27;&#x27; ou \uline{multistrates}</code> | <code>\tikz[baseline=-.4ex,x=.75em,y=.75em]{\draw (.5,.5) circle (.48);\draw (.18,.48)--(.82,.48) (.4,.2)--(.65,.8) (.32,.76)--(.72,.23);} $M={}$ ensemble des \unsure{``}\uline{figures} $\subset\mathscr F$ \uline{élémentaires}&#x27;&#x27; ou \uline{multistrates}</code> | The circled cross/asterisk is drawn as in B002. ### Remaining uncertainties

- Opening tuple, cancelled X≤: <code>X≤</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;underlying letters/signs obscured by hatching&#x27;]</code>; low. Every stroke is not plain; the deleted doubt is not resolved.
- a), cancelled universal and final être: <code>∀x∈M ... être</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;case within the crossed formula remains indistinct&#x27;, &#x27;final word partly obscured&#x27;]</code>; low. Crossing covers portions of the small letters; C’s deleted doubts remain intact.
- a), supralinear word: <code>posant</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;compressed internal letters not wholly separable&#x27;]</code>; medium. The word is longer than pour and a p/o/s-like beginning and terminal stroke are visible, but every internal letter is not plain.
- a), supralinear word, cancellation affiliation: <code>physically live insertion attached to a cancelled formula</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;an insertion abandoned with the surrounding formula&#x27;]</code>; medium. The hatching does not cross its letters, but its leader attaches to the deleted formula; physical and textual abandonment are distinct questions.
- a), cancelled sign before réunion: <code>∃</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;overwritten initial strokes not fully separable&#x27;]</code>; low. Retain the C doubt because the sign is under dark correction.
- Small index/exponent x forms: M≤x, later F^x, tilde Y^x: <code>x</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;X&#x27;]</code>; low. The small crosses lack independent case features. The doubt is marked at the first such live occurrence and applies to the later small exponents, not to plainly large main-line X.
- Initial left margin, formées ... éléments: <code>de</code>; alternatives <code>[&quot;d&#x27;&quot;]</code>; low. A d-loop and low following stroke are clear; high apostrophe and a separate final s are not.
- Deleted At before Les axiomes: <code>At</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;A/t partly obscured&#x27;]</code>; medium. Both general letter shapes are visible but not every part survives the strike; do not remove the deleted C doubt.
- Ramène line, textual state: <code>ordinary baseline</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;later interlinear addition represented as an insertion by C&#x27;]</code>; medium. No caret enters the sentence. Its smaller hand/position may reflect an afterthought, but physical insertion is not established.
- Earlier digit under final At 6: <code>5</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;obscured earlier digit&#x27;]</code>; low. An old horizontal stroke and loop remain, but not the whole earlier digit.
- Minimum-stratum set, overwritten relation: <code>cancelled ≤ before live ≪</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;indeterminate overwritten sign&#x27;]</code>; low. Extra strokes under the live double angle do not expose the old relation completely.
- After minimum-stratum set: <code>,</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;.&#x27;]</code>; low. The single small oblique punctuation mark cannot be securely assigned by shape alone.
- At 5 marginal note, Si G,H insertion state: <code>supralinear insertion</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;ordinary continuation crowded above the struck row&#x27;]</code>; medium. The live group is visibly above the cancelled row, but its exact attachment has no unambiguous caret.
- At 5 marginal note, noun before d’une: <code>sous-figures</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;strates&#x27;]</code>; low. The small prefix/word group is crowded against other strokes. The first C candidate remains but is not settled.
- At 5 marginal caption, extrinsic/intrinsic qualifier: <code>extrinsèq.</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;intrinsèq.&#x27;]</code>; medium. The existing crop was re-viewed after a targeted query: an e-like initial loop and crossing stroke before tall t favor extrinsèq.; the crowded beginning remains open. Earlier intrinsic-only alternatives are superseded.
- At 5 marginal caption, parentheses: <code>caption enclosed in parentheses</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;left/right curves could include connector strokes&#x27;]</code>; medium. The curved marks flank the underlined caption, but crowding with the adjacent leaders warrants second reading.
- Earlier digit under final At 5: <code>6</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;obscured earlier digit&#x27;]</code>; low. Heavy surcharge prevents complete recovery; retain C’s old-digit doubt.
- Earlier digit under final At 7: <code>6</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;obscured earlier digit&#x27;]</code>; low. The lower ink mass is too dense to recover every stroke; retain the old-digit doubt.
- At 7 middle refinement decoration: <code>filled dot</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;small partly closed circle&#x27;]</code>; low. The middle mark is much smaller and darker than the clear hollow circles on either side, with possibly a small open top.
- At_M 2, crossed beginning before partie: <code>pa</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;partly overwritten beginning&#x27;]</code>; low. Old and new p/a strokes overlap; the cancelled candidate must remain doubtful.
- At_M 2, Ψ baseline state: <code>ordinary Ψ</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;a correction/overwriting not recoverable from the extra foot stroke&#x27;]</code>; medium. Its vertical level agrees with the other capitals and no caret is visible; a diagonal remnant alone is not an insertion marker.
- At_M 3, crossed group: <code>{X,Y}</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;underlying brace/letter group partly obscured&#x27;]</code>; low. The hatching prevents complete recovery; the C doubt remains.
- Opening tuple, abbreviated relation word: <code>rel b.</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;rel\\textsuperscript{tion}&#x27;]</code>; low. cancelled X/inequality/compatibility knot remains uncertain but contains legible crossed strokes, so retain partial deleted formula instead of replacing all by illegible. CHANGE A03: opening explanatory relation label is abbreviated, with rel and a tall looped character followed by a point and comma-like mark. Replace expanded relation alternative by rel b. versus rel with raised tion, retain the uncertainty and add the visible separating comma. The following word is reflexive, followed by sym; no separate et is secure.
- Initial left margin, preposition before compatibility symbol: <code>pour</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;p. r.&#x27;]</code>; low. retain the de/d' uncertainty and the complete marginal definition. CHANGE A04: the short bottom preposition is compressed into p plus point/low strokes; pour and p. r. remain possible, so mark both rather than claim a fully written word.
- At 3, deleted small group: <code>x</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;de&#x27;]</code>; low. CHANGE A05: a left loop/ascender and right curved return survive across the strike; neither a single crossed x nor a compressed de is secure. Preserve both alternatives within deletion.
- Minimum-stratum marginal note, article before plus: <code>la</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;le&#x27;]</code>; low. retain petite strate, not Atome: long s followed by t-r-a-t-e is visible. CHANGE A06: article before plus has a flattened exit that admits la/le. CHANGE A07: add the short headless arch and detached dash above Axiome; the already represented rising connector arrow remains. The arch is separate ink from letters and from the Corollaire bracket.
- Definition arrow label: <code>dfn</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;df&#x27;, &#x27;defn&#x27;, &#x27;défn&#x27;, &#x27;déf&#x27;]</code>; low. retain the literal X as least element, although the next line gives the mathematical reason to question it; ink stays X. CHANGE A08: label contains d and tall f, whose right return could be final n or just f's exit; add df among the existing abbreviation alternatives. No full-word expansion is made. Circles, primes, triangle and equality agree.
- At 5 marginal note, crossed word: <code>Axiome</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;underlying word uncertain under heavy hatching&#x27;]</code>; low. CHANGE A11 as above, tentative Axiome under the crossing; not a resolved reading. Live lower sous-figures and figure F are separate from this row, not an inserted F,G group.

### Witness S

- SA21-K7-01: Confirmed as an observation of the literal triangular hypothesis; the mathematical replacement is kept out of the transcription and qualified in N006-At6 below. scan006_at6_formula.png, original box [419,1144,1637,1295], shows a closed left-pointing triangle in X triangleleft F and the explicit parenthetical X∈tilde F. It also shows the universal quantifier struck. The final live numeral is6 above uncertain5 (scan006_at6_number.png [266,1139,399,1246]). A nontrivial intended version needs X∈M, F∈the figure set, X≪F; merely replacing the relation while leaving arbitrary X∈the figure set loses the stratum typing.

### Remaining uncertainties

- cancelled opening axiom, Phi: <code>\Phi</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;\\varphi&#x27;]</code>; medium. A vertical staff and horizontal foot favour capital Phi, but cancellation overlaps the bowl; C doubt not resolved.
- cancelled opening axiom, final word: <code>élément</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. Parts of the last letters intersect the strike; not every letter is plain.
- margin overwritten numeral: <code>underlying 6, final 5</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;underlying numeral not separable&#x27;]</code>; low. Retained C deleted-numeral doubt; overwrite is clear but underlying identity is not.
- cancelled Posant: <code>Posant</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;Posons&#x27;]</code>; medium. Tall last stroke favours t, but intervening cancellation prevents certainty.
- cancelled first arrow: <code>\Longleftrightarrow</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;\\Longrightarrow&#x27;]</code>; low. Two shafts are visible, but the left end meets the strike and interlinear formula.
- label on cancelled first arrow: <code>defn</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;déf&#x27;]</code>; low. The label is directly above the double-shaft arrow. A d-like loop, descending f and small final returning stroke favour an abbreviated defn; the crossed joins and possible accent are not all plain. Neither the full word défini nor a certain three-letter label is warranted. Its placement above the arrow is clear.
- letter after cancelled labelled arrow: <code>Y</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;X&#x27;]</code>; low. Heavy crossed fork at the cancellation intersection does not discriminate the lower arm.
- cancelled contient: <code>contient</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Word outline and tall t strokes survive, but its middle is crossed.
- cancelled interlinear est/ent: <code>est</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;ent&#x27;]</code>; low. The short word has an ending tall stroke, while the middle is crossed.
- cancelled interlinear minimal/minimum: <code>minimal</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;minimum&#x27;]</code>; low. Ending intersects cancellation; live minimal elsewhere is not used to settle the cancelled occurrence.
- cancelled plus petit élément: <code>plus petit élément</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. Several underlying letters remain visible, but the broad strike hides enough strokes to mark the whole candidate doubtful.
- cancelled lower formula after the live posons insertion: <code>i.e.</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Small introductory letters and dots lie directly under the strike; retained C doubt.
- At_M 6 overwritten label: <code>underlying 5, final 6</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;underlying numeral not separable&#x27;]</code>; low. Dark final 6 overlays angular remnants; old digit not fully legible.
- At_M 5 overwritten label: <code>underlying 6, final 5</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;underlying numeral not separable&#x27;]</code>; low. Dark final 5 overlays rounded remnants; old digit not fully legible.
- cancelled At_M (tf) label: <code>1</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;0&#x27;]</code>; low. Small dark strokes before (tf) are partly merged with parentheses/cancellation; C numeral remains provisional.
- cancelled group before page 5: <code>At</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Dense diagonal cancelling strokes obscure the group; initial A partly survives.
- slanted marginal implication sign: <code>two horizontal shafts, upper one with rounded downturned end</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;\\Longrightarrow&#x27;]</code>; medium. The upper shaft turns down rather than forming a clear angular arrowhead; rounded shape retained in TikZ with the C uncertainty.
- Omb argument in Mulomb definition: <code>x</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;X&#x27;]</code>; medium. Smaller than the neighbouring capital X but with identical crossed-stroke construction; primary C retained with explicit case doubt.
- final M subscript in Omb definition: <code>x</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;X&#x27;]</code>; low. Reduced subscript size removes a secure case criterion; do not normalize to the capital used elsewhere in the formula.
- spacieux in final parenthesis: <code>spacieux</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;spécieux&#x27;]</code>; medium. Faint a/e letter favours C; absence of a visible accent alone does not wholly settle the vowel.
- linear ordering of the cancelled drafts at the start of a): <code>C’s local sequence, with interlinear additions marked and déf moved to its arrow</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;different drafting sequence of the cancelled lines&#x27;]</code>; low. Two interlinear cancelled fragments partly overlap the baseline draft; source position is visible but a unique temporal order is not certified.
- quand on à/a: <code>à</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;a&#x27;]</code>; medium. A detached slant stands above the compact letter. The normal syntax would require a, but is not used to erase that visible stroke. Crop crops/scan007_general_case.png [523,972,1666,1164].
- cancelled opening axiom, participle before Phi superscript X: <code>posant</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;posons&#x27;]</code>; low. Same M hand as quantified ambient M; do not remap axiom subscripts to Greek mu. Cancelled Phi/varphi remains uncertain. Add posant&#124;posons uncertainty: cancellation crosses the final n/t/s group. Final élément remains one uncertain word; no separate abbreviation dot or additional word is established. Formula agrees. Correct the accidental TeX control sequence `\inM` to `\in M` as syntax only (no changed ink place).
- D’autre part paragraph, verb after on: <code>laisser</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;laisse&#x27;]</code>; low. On and laisser are separated by blank baseline space with no independent p-e-u-t sequence; the tall strokes crossing that interval belong to the lower inserted profit. Omit peut. The flattened terminal of laisser does not securely distinguish r from an exit, so mark laisser/laisse. The rightmost F modifier meets the physical image edge: its surviving left stroke cannot securely distinguish tilde from overbar; mark both. Remplacer retains its final hooked r and de la retains d loop, tall l and a exit. The two punctuation marks between At1 and At3 are a semicolon (confirmed in B030). Font/M mapping and insertion au profit agree.
- family modifier after élément de structure: <code>\widetilde{\mathscr F}</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;\\overline{\\mathscr F}&#x27;]</code>; low. On and laisser are separated by blank baseline space with no independent p-e-u-t sequence; the tall strokes crossing that interval belong to the lower inserted profit. Omit peut. The flattened terminal of laisser does not securely distinguish r from an exit, so mark laisser/laisse. The rightmost F modifier meets the physical image edge: its surviving left stroke cannot securely distinguish tilde from overbar; mark both. Remplacer retains its final hooked r and de la retains d loop, tall l and a exit. The two punctuation marks between At1 and At3 are a semicolon (confirmed in B030). Font/M mapping and insertion au profit agree.
- first live reference to page 5, abbreviation after V.: <code>p</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;p.&#x27;]</code>; low. Read V. followed by p/p. uncertainty and 5: separate deep V and a short following point, then looped p group. No full page sequence. The p terminal is joined/flattened, so an independent abbreviation point cannot be settled.
- cancelled parenthesized axiom qualifier: <code>tf</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;Af&#x27;, &#x27;4f&#x27;]</code>; low. Retain uncertain 1/0 in the dark numeral/parenthesis superposition. The first character of the parenthesized qualifier is crossed and its low triangular/looped shape is not secure: mark tf/Af/4f rather than assert tf or omit the whole qualifier. Second letter has tall f loop and descender.
- cancelled definition words: <code>formées t.q.</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;fermées t.q.; second abbreviation remains uncertain&#x27;]</code>; low. Add fermées alternative to cancelled formées (closed first-vowel loop interrupted by strike). Cancelled t.q. retains t cross and lower loop, so uncertain reading is better than wholly illegible. Two independent punctuation marks after inserted telles que remain as colon; do not drop punctuation.
- cancelled sign before Omb(x): <code>X</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;\\Phi&#x27;]</code>; low. Retain Mulomb. A horizontal cancellation traverses the small où group. Crossed sign retains X strokes, but upper loop also admits Phi, so add Phi as an alternative. Small x in Omb has reduced strokes, and case cannot be settled by expected mathematics; retain x/X. Last X, tilde F and set brace agree.

### Remaining uncertainties

- en termes/terme: <code>termes</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;terme&#x27;]</code>; medium. The low final run is not cleanly segmented into an independently identifiable final s.
- NB struck abbreviation: <code>i.e.</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. Letters and stops are crossed by a dark horizontal strike; retain the C doubt.
- NB struck article: <code>la</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. The looped initial and dense ending are overinked; every letter is not plain.
- NB d’axiome(s): <code>d&#x27;axiome</code>; alternatives <code>[&quot;d&#x27;axiomes&quot;]</code>; low. The word reaches the sheet edge, and the terminal run does not securely identify or exclude s.
- At heading Soient: <code>Soient</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; medium. The ending is overinked and the global cancellation crosses the word region; not every letter is plain enough to resolve a C doubt in cancelled text.
- At local deletion before K: <code>et</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. The short word is largely reduced to a thick horizontal/crossed shape.
- At local deletion before Supposons: <code>Il</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. The two-letter-like upright/loop cluster is densely overinked.
- At display deleted initial quantifier: <code>\forall</code>; alternatives <code>[]</code>; low. Several heavy strokes obscure the original sign; ∀ is a candidate, not a resolved sign.
- Cor first prior Sup operand: <code>\widetilde X</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;X&#x27;]</code>; low. The prior crossing strokes are mixed with the final F and the descending Sup; a prior tilde cannot be separated.
- Cor second prior Sup operand: <code>\widetilde Y</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;Y&#x27;]</code>; low. Old strokes remain inside the final G and its wave; whether the prior Y carried a tilde is not plain.
- Cor prior letter after ∀X,: <code>\varphi</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;y&#x27;]</code>; low. The lower loop and upper crossings are overlaid, leaving the prior glyph ambiguous.
- Cor surviving letter after ∀X,: <code>Y</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;y&#x27;]</code>; low. The surviving letter is compressed and overlaid; its case is not independently secure.
- Slanted margin, cancellation scope: <code>live complete note</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;whole note cancelled&#x27;]</code>; low. One of the long diagonal block strikes crosses the slanted note; it does not independently establish the intended cancellation extent. The alternatives are now encoded in place, with the complete wording retained in both.
- Quoted definition, second named letter: <code>G</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;C&#x27;]</code>; low. Open bowl with returning terminal but no distinct interior bar; local use of G supplies the primary candidate without making the stroke certain.
- First definition, operand after parallel sign: <code>G</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;C&#x27;]</code>; medium. More sharply inward lower return than the subsequent clear C-shaped intersection operand; still lacks the full internal bar of the next G.
- Cancelled Cor, intersection operand: <code>C</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;G&#x27;]</code>; low. Open C contour plus an additional upper-left interior stroke; neither certain G nor uncorrected simple C is established.
- Cancelled Cor, surviving second Sup operand: <code>\widetilde G</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;\\widetilde C&#x27;]</code>; low. The wave is visible but older strokes interfere with the internal portion of the rounded letter.
- Opening facultatif(s), final letter: <code>facultatifs</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;facultatif&#x27;]</code>; low. The terminal loop after the tall f is compressed; a separately articulated final s is not secure. Retain the quoted word with uncertainty on the final s, facultatif\unsure{s}. The two high quotation ticks remain unchanged.
- First equivalence label: <code>dfn</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;defn&#x27;, &#x27;def&#x27;]</code>; low. Retain the whole formula, including the locally C-shaped intersection operand, the crossed compatibility graphic, and slashed-oval empty-figure sign. The label has a looped d, tall narrow stroke and short terminal run; add def as an alternative to dfn/defn, rather than certifying the blind reader's def. The first G/C remains doubtful and the middle barred G remains G; mathematics does not supply an absent internal bar.
- NB overwritten underlying ending: <code>tera</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;old ending partly obscured&#x27;]</code>; low. The live r, accented e, s/u run and tall l precede the correction. The upper t staff and e loop support replacement te; the underlying longer ending remains tentative tera. The cancellation does not extend over the entire word. This is the same verb change as B011.
- NB cancelled middle phrase: <code>c&#x27;est un</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;word sequence not fully recoverable under strike&#x27;]</code>; low. The horizontal strike crosses the initial dotted abbreviation, a c/apostrophe/low-letter sequence and a short u/n-like run, then a reverse-E/bracket-shaped end. Retain i.e. as tentative; mark c'est un tentative, and extend the final symbol alternatives to exists/closing bracket. The blind rien is not established by the initial curved c. These are one cancelled-phrase place, also visible in B011.
- NB struck final mathematical symbol: <code>\exists</code>; alternatives <code>[&#x27;]&#x27;]</code>; low. The horizontal strike crosses the initial dotted abbreviation, a c/apostrophe/low-letter sequence and a short u/n-like run, then a reverse-E/bracket-shaped end. Retain i.e. as tentative; mark c'est un tentative, and extend the final symbol alternatives to exists/closing bracket. The blind rien is not established by the initial curved c. These are one cancelled-phrase place, also visible in B011.
